Essai sur la nature du commerce en général (Richard Cantillon, 1755)
==========================================================
The first systematic treatise on economics: value, money, and the entrepreneur. First edition, 1755. In French; no public-domain English translation exists. Public domain. Sourced from Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_essai-sur-la-nature-du-c_cantillon-richard_1755).
